Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The atmospheres variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 661 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 480 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the atmospheres decomposition. By minimality, the element a^661 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 480-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
